Then pointers = base + f*cap.\n\nIn kernel:\n```\nunsigned long long idx = (unsigned long long)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\nunsigned long long stride = (unsigned long long)gridDim.x * blockDim.x;\nfor (; idx < n; idx += stride) {\n  const double S=spot[idx], K=strike[idx], rr=r[idx], qq=q[idx], T=t[idx], sigma=vol[idx];\n  if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) { out[idx]=0.0; continue; }\n  const double sqrtT = sqrt(T);\n  const double d1 = (log(S/K) + (rr - qq + 0.5*sigma*sigma)*T)/(sigma*sqrtT);\n  const double d2 = d1 - sigma*sqrtT;\n  const double Nd1 = 0.5*(1.0+erf(d1*BS_SQRT1_2));\n  const double Nd2 = 0.5*(1.0+erf(d2*BS_SQRT1_2));\n  const double discount = exp(-rr*T);\n  const double qdisc = exp(-qq*T);\n  double price;\n  if (type[idx] == 0.0) price = S*qdisc*Nd1 - K*discount*Nd2;\n  else price = K*discount*(0.5*(1.0+erf(-d2*BS_SQRT1_2))) - S*qdisc*(0.5*(1.0+erf(-d1*BS_SQRT1_2)));\n  out[idx]=price;\n}\n```\nCould avoid computing put's N(-) via erf again using 1-Nd: N(-d) = 1-N(d) exactly mathematically; in floating may differ slightly.
